System-Level Diagram\n    12→\n    13→```mermaid\n    14→flowchart LR\n    15→    subgraph Browser / Mobile\n    16→        A[SPA<br/>(React/Next.js)]\n    17→    end\n    18→\n    19→    subgraph Edge\n    20→        AGW[API Gateway<br/>(Node.js + Fastify)]\n    21→    end\n    22→\n    23→    subgraph Core Services\n    24→        Auth[Auth Service]\n    25→        Users[User Service]\n    26→        Courses[Course Service]\n    27→        Events[Event Dispatcher]\n    28→        Notif[Notification Service]\n    29→        Reco[Recommendation Service]\n    30→        Search[Search Indexer]\n    31→    end\n    32→\n    33→    subgraph Infra\n    34→        Kafka[(Kafka/NATS)]\n    35→        DB[(PostgreSQL)]\n    36→        Redis[(Redis)]\n    37→        S3[(Object Storage)]\n    38→        Prom[(Prometheus + Grafana)]\n    39→    end\n    40→\n    41→    A -- REST/WebSocket --> AGW\n    42→    AGW -- JWT --> Auth\n    43→    AGW -- RPC --> Users & Courses\n    44→    AGW -- Publish Domain Events --> Kafka\n    45→    Kafka --> Events\n    46→    Events --> Notif & Reco & Search\n    47→    DB <--> Users & Courses\n    48→    Redis <--> Notif\n    49→    S3 <--> Courses\n    50→    Prom --> Grafana\n    51→```\n    52→\n    53→---\n    54→\n    55→## 2. Layered Component Model\n    56→\n    57→| Layer             | Responsibility                                  | Protocols                           |\n    58→| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- |\n    59→| Presentation      | React SPA, WebSocket client, SSR pages          | `HTTPS`, `WSS`                      |\n    60→| API Gateway       | Rate-limit, authN, authZ, request enrichment    | `HTTP/2`, `gRPC` (internal)         |\n    61→| Service Layer     | Business logic, orchestration, validation       | `gRPC`, `Kafka`                     |\n    62→| Repository Layer  | Data mappers, ORM (Prisma), caching             | `SQL`, `Redis`                      |\n    63→| Event Backbone    | Guaranteed, ordered domain event propagation    | `Kafka`, `NATS JetStream`           |\n    64→\n    65→---\n    66→\n    67→## 3. Technology Stack\n    68→\n    69→| Concern            | Choice                         | Rationale                                              |\n    70→| ------------------ | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------ |\n    71→| Runtime            | Node.js (v18 LTS)              | Native ESM, top-level `await`, diagnostics channel     |\n    72→| Web Framework      | Fastify (4.x)                  | High-performance, schema-driven, hooks                 |\n    73→| ORM                | Prisma (5.x)                   | Type-safe, migrations as code                          |\n    74→| Message Bus        | Kafka (3.x) / NATS (2.x)       | At-least-once delivery, back-pressure control          |\n    75→| Auth               | Clerk (OIDC) + JWT             | Social logins, MFA, SSO                                |\n    76→| Observability      | OpenTelemetry + Prometheus     | Distributed tracing & metrics                          |\n    77→| Containerization   | Docker + Helm                  | K8s ready, GitOps deploys                              |\n    78→| CI/CD              | GitHub Actions, Renovate       | SemVer releases, automated dependency updates          |\n    79→\n    80→---\n    81→\n    82→## 4. Domain Event Contract (TypeScript)\n    83→\n    84→```ts\n    85→/**\n    86→ * Base interface for all domain events.\n    87→ * Each event must be serializable and contain a unique ID\n    88→ * to guarantee idempotent consumption by downstream services.\n    89→ */\n    90→export interface DomainEvent<T extends Record<string, unknown> = unknown> {\n    91→  readonly id: string;                  // ULID\n    92→  readonly name: string;                // e.g. \"AssignmentSubmitted\"\n    93→  readonly occurredAt: string;          // ISO-8601 timestamp\n    94→  readonly aggregateId: string;         // Entity root ID (e.g. assignmentId)\n    95→  readonly payload: T;                  // Raw event data\n    96→  readonly version: number;             // Schema evolution support\n    97→}\n    98→```\n    99→\n   100→---\n   101→\n   102→## 5. Event Emitter (Service ➜ Kafka)\n   103→\n   104→```ts\n   105→import { Kafka, Producer } from \"kafkajs\";\n   106→import { DomainEvent } from \"../types/events\";\n   107→\n   108→export class EventEmitter {\n   109→  private readonly producer: Producer;\n   110→\n   111→  constructor(private readonly kafka: Kafka) {\n   112→    this.producer = kafka.producer({ allowAutoTopicCreation: false });\n   113→  }\n   114→\n   115→  async start(): Promise<void> {\n   116→    await this.producer.connect();\n   117→  }\n   118→\n   119→  async stop(): Promise<void> {\n   120→    await this.producer.disconnect();\n   121→  }\n   122→\n   123→  /**\n   124→   * Publish a domain event to the message bus.\n   125→   * Retries are handled by kafkajs with exponential back-off.\n   126→   */\n   127→  async emit(event: DomainEvent): Promise<void> {\n   128→    await this.producer.send({\n   129→      topic: `domain.${event.name}`,\n   130→      messages: [\n   131→        {\n   132→          key: event.aggregateId,\n   133→          value: JSON.stringify(event),\n   134→          headers: { \"x-event-version\": `${event.version}` },\n   135→        },\n   136→      ],\n   137→    });\n   138→  }\n   139→}\n   140→```\n   141→\n   142→---\n   143→\n   144→## 6. Event Consumer Skeleton\n   145→\n   146→```ts\n   147→import { Kafka, EachMessagePayload } from \"kafkajs\";\n   148→import { logger } from \"../utils/logger\";\n   149→import { processAssignmentSubmitted } from \"./handlers/assignmentSubmitted\";\n   150→\n   151→const TOPIC = \"domain.AssignmentSubmitted\";\n   152→\n   153→export async function createAssignmentSubmittedConsumer(kafka: Kafka) {\n   154→  const consumer = kafka.consumer({ groupId: \"notification-service\" });\n   155→\n   156→  await consumer.subscribe({ topic: TOPIC, fromBeginning: false });\n   157→\n   158→  await consumer.run({\n   159→    autoCommit: false,\n   160→    eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n   161→      try {\n   162→        const { message, heartbeat, commitOffsetsIfNecessary } = payload;\n   163→        const event = JSON.parse(message.value!.toString());\n   164→\n   165→        await processAssignmentSubmitted(event);\n   166→\n   167→        await commitOffsetsIfNecessary();\n   168→        await heartbeat(); // keep consumer session alive\n   169→      } catch (error) {\n   170→        logger.error(error, \"Failed to process AssignmentSubmitted\");\n   171→        // Optionally forward to a dead-letter topic for inspection\n   172→      }\n   173→    },\n   174→  });\n   175→\n   176→  return consumer;\n   177→}\n   178→```\n   179→\n   180→---\n   181→\n   182→## 7. Repository Pattern (Prisma + Caching)\n   183→\n   184→```ts\n   185→import { PrismaClient, User } from \"@prisma/client\";\n   186→import Redis from \"ioredis\";\n   187→import ms from \"ms\";\n   188→\n   189→export class UserRepository {\n   190→  private readonly cacheTtl = ms(\"10m\") / 1000; // seconds\n   191→\n   192→  constructor(\n   193→    private readonly prisma: PrismaClient,\n   194→    private readonly redis: Redis\n   195→  ) {}\n   196→\n   197→  private cacheKey(userId: string): string {\n   198→    return `user:${userId}`;\n   199→  }\n   200→\n   201→  async findById(userId: string): Promise<User | null> {\n   202→    // 1. Check Redis\n   203→    const cached = await this.redis.get(this.cacheKey(userId));\n   204→    if (cached) return JSON.parse(cached) as User;\n   205→\n   206→    // 2. Fallback to DB\n   207→    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId } });\n   208→    if (user) {\n   209→      await this.redis.set(\n   210→        this.cacheKey(userId),\n   211→        JSON.stringify(user),\n   212→        \"EX\",\n   213→        this.cacheTtl\n   214→      );\n   215→    }\n   216→    return user;\n   217→  }\n   218→\n   219→  async create(data: Omit<User, \"id\">): Promise<User> {\n   220→    const user = await this.prisma.user.create({ data });\n   221→    await this.redis.del(this.cacheKey(user.id)); // bust cache\n   222→    return user;\n   223→  }\n   224→}\n   225→```\n   226→\n   227→---\n   228→\n   229→## 8. Example Service (Notification Service)\n   230→\n   231→```ts\n   232→import { EventEmitter } from \"../events/eventEmitter\";\n   233→import { NotificationRepository } from \"../repositories/notificationRepo\";\n   234→import { DomainEvent } from \"../types/events\";\n   235→\n   236→export class NotificationService {\n   237→  constructor(\n   238→    private readonly repo: NotificationRepository,\n   239→    private readonly emitter: EventEmitter\n   240→  ) {}\n   241→\n   242→  /**\n   243→   * Create a user notification and emit a follow-up domain event.\n   244→   */\n   245→  async notify(event: DomainEvent<AssignmentPayload>): Promise<void> {\n   246→    // Business invariant\n   247→    if (!event.payload.recipientId) {\n   248→      throw new Error(\"Recipient ID missing in AssignmentSubmitted event\");\n   249→    }\n   250→\n   251→    const notification = await this.repo.create({\n   252→      userId: event.payload.recipientId,\n   253→      title: \"New Assignment Submitted\",\n   254→      body: `${event.payload.authorName} has submitted an assignment.`,\n   255→    });\n   256→\n   257→    await this.emitter.emit({\n   258→      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n   259→      name: \"NotificationCreated\",\n   260→      occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n   261→      aggregateId: notification.id,\n   262→      payload: { notificationId: notification.id },\n   263→      version: 1,\n   264→    });\n   265→  }\n   266→}\n   267→```\n   268→\n   269→---\n   270→\n   271→## 9. Security Considerations\n   272→\n   273→1. **JWT validation** is enforced at the API Gateway with public JWK rotation every 60 minutes.  \n   274→2. **TLS 1.3** is mandatory for all edge traffic; internal traffic uses mTLS between micro-services.  \n   275→3. **CSP, HSTS, and CSRF** protection headers set by Fastify `helmet` plugin.  \n   276→4. **OPA-based policy engine** scopes admin operations (audit logs, PII access).  \n   277→\n   278→---\n   279→\n   280→## 10. System-Level Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Browser / Mobile\n        A[SPA<br/>(React/Next.js)]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Edge\n        AGW[API Gateway<br/>(Node.js + Fastify)]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Core Services\n        Auth[Auth Service]\n        Users[User Service]\n        Courses[Course Service]\n        Events[Event Dispatcher]\n        Notif[Notification Service]\n        Reco[Recommendation Service]\n        Search[Search Indexer]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Infra\n        Kafka[(Kafka/NATS)]\n        DB[(PostgreSQL)]\n        Redis[(Redis)]\n        S3[(Object Storage)]\n        Prom[(Prometheus + Grafana)]\n    end\n\n    A -- REST/WebSocket --> AGW\n    AGW -- JWT --> Auth\n    AGW -- RPC --> Users & Courses\n    AGW -- Publish Domain Events --> Kafka\n    Kafka --> Events\n    Events --> Notif & Reco & Search\n    DB <--> Users & Courses\n    Redis <--> Notif\n    S3 <--> Courses\n    Prom --> Grafana\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Layered Component Model\n\n| Layer             | Responsibility                                  | Protocols                           |\n| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- |\n| Presentation      | React SPA, WebSocket client, SSR pages          | `HTTPS`, `WSS`                      |\n| API Gateway       | Rate-limit, authN, authZ, request enrichment    | `HTTP/2`, `gRPC` (internal)         |\n| Service Layer     | Business logic, orchestration, validation       | `gRPC`, `Kafka`                     |\n| Repository Layer  | Data mappers, ORM (Prisma), caching             | `SQL`, `Redis`                      |\n| Event Backbone    | Guaranteed, ordered domain event propagation    | `Kafka`, `NATS JetStream`           |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Domain Event Contract (TypeScript)\n\n```ts\n/**\n * Base interface for all domain events.\n * Each event must be serializable and contain a unique ID\n * to guarantee idempotent consumption by downstream services.\n */\nexport interface DomainEvent<T extends Record<string, unknown> = unknown> {\n  readonly id: string;                  // ULID\n  readonly name: string;                // e.g. \"AssignmentSubmitted\"\n  readonly occurredAt: string;          // ISO-8601 timestamp\n  readonly aggregateId: string;         // Entity root ID (e.g. assignmentId)\n  readonly payload: T;                  // Raw event data\n  readonly version: number;             // Schema evolution support\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Event Emitter (Service ➜ Kafka)\n\n```ts\nimport { Kafka, Producer } from \"kafkajs\";\nimport { DomainEvent } from \"../types/events\";\n\nexport class EventEmitter {\n  private readonly producer: Producer;\n\n  constructor(private readonly kafka: Kafka) {\n    this.producer = kafka.producer({ allowAutoTopicCreation: false });\n  }\n\n  async start(): Promise<void> {\n    await this.producer.connect();\n  }\n\n  async stop(): Promise<void> {\n    await this.producer.disconnect();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Publish a domain event to the message bus.\n   * Retries are handled by kafkajs with exponential back-off.\n   */\n  async emit(event: DomainEvent): Promise<void> {\n    await this.producer.send({\n      topic: `domain.${event.name}`,\n      messages: [\n        {\n          key: event.aggregateId,\n          value: JSON.stringify(event),\n          headers: { \"x-event-version\": `${event.version}` },\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Event Consumer Skeleton\n\n```ts\nimport { Kafka, EachMessagePayload } from \"kafkajs\";\nimport { logger } from \"../utils/logger\";\nimport { processAssignmentSubmitted } from \"./handlers/assignmentSubmitted\";\n\nconst TOPIC = \"domain.AssignmentSubmitted\";\n\nexport async function createAssignmentSubmittedConsumer(kafka: Kafka) {\n  const consumer = kafka.consumer({ groupId: \"notification-service\" });\n\n  await consumer.subscribe({ topic: TOPIC, fromBeginning: false });\n\n  await consumer.run({\n    autoCommit: false,\n    eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n      try {\n        const { message, heartbeat, commitOffsetsIfNecessary } = payload;\n        const event = JSON.parse(message.value!.toString());\n\n        await processAssignmentSubmitted(event);\n\n        await commitOffsetsIfNecessary();\n        await heartbeat(); // keep consumer session alive\n      } catch (error) {\n        logger.error(error, \"Failed to process AssignmentSubmitted\");\n        // Optionally forward to a dead-letter topic for inspection\n      }\n    },\n  });\n\n  return consumer;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Repository Pattern (Prisma + Caching)\n\n```ts\nimport { PrismaClient, User } from \"@prisma/client\";\nimport Redis from \"ioredis\";\nimport ms from \"ms\";\n\nexport class UserRepository {\n  private readonly cacheTtl = ms(\"10m\") / 1000; // seconds\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly prisma: PrismaClient,\n    private readonly redis: Redis\n  ) {}\n\n  private cacheKey(userId: string): string {\n    return `user:${userId}`;\n  }\n\n  async findById(userId: string): Promise<User | null> {\n    // 1. Check Redis\n    const cached = await this.redis.get(this.cacheKey(userId));\n    if (cached) return JSON.parse(cached) as User;\n\n    // 2. Fallback to DB\n    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId } });\n    if (user) {\n      await this.redis.set(\n        this.cacheKey(userId),\n        JSON.stringify(user),\n        \"EX\",\n        this.cacheTtl\n      );\n    }\n    return user;\n  }\n\n  async create(data: Omit<User, \"id\">): Promise<User> {\n    const user = await this.prisma.user.create({ data });\n    await this.redis.del(this.cacheKey(user.id)); // bust cache\n    return user;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Example Service (Notification Service)\n\n```ts\nimport { EventEmitter } from \"../events/eventEmitter\";\nimport { NotificationRepository } from \"../repositories/notificationRepo\";\nimport { DomainEvent } from \"../types/events\";\n\nexport class NotificationService {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly repo: NotificationRepository,\n    private readonly emitter: EventEmitter\n  ) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Create a user notification and emit a follow-up domain event.\n   */\n  async notify(event: DomainEvent<AssignmentPayload>): Promise<void> {\n    // Business invariant\n    if (!event.payload.recipientId) {\n      throw new Error(\"Recipient ID missing in AssignmentSubmitted event\");\n    }\n\n    const notification = await this.repo.create({\n      userId: event.payload.recipientId,\n      title: \"New Assignment Submitted\",\n      body: `${event.payload.authorName} has submitted an assignment.`,\n    });\n\n    await this.emitter.emit({\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      name: \"NotificationCreated\",\n      occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      aggregateId: notification.id,\n      payload: { notificationId: notification.id },\n      version: 1,\n    });\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. Security Considerations\n\n1. **JWT validation** is enforced at the API Gateway with public JWK rotation every 60 minutes.  \n2. **TLS 1.3** is mandatory for all edge traffic; internal traffic uses mTLS between micro-services.  \n3. **CSP, HSTS, and CSRF** protection headers set by Fastify `helmet` plugin.  \n4. **OPA-based policy engine** scopes admin operations (audit logs, PII access).  \n\n---\n\n## 10. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013Rz2DGwxtj89emPY7jB2yu","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# ADR-002: Introduce Event Sourcing with Kafka\n     3→\n     4→* Status: **Accepted**  \n     5→* Date: 2024-05-13  \n     6→* Decision Makers: Core Platform Team (PulseLearn Campus Hub)  \n     7→* Supersedes: *N/A*  \n     8→* References: PCH-RFC-11, ADR-001 (Adopt Micro-Service Architecture)  \n     9→\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→## 1. Context ‑ Why change?\n    13→\n    14→PulseLearn Campus Hub (PCH) is experiencing rapid feature growth:\n    15→\n    16→* Real-time dashboards for quiz progress, peer-review feedback, and badge awards  \n    17→* A new gamification engine that recalculates points on every learning event  \n    18→* Audit-ready reporting for compliance and accreditation bodies  \n    19→\n    20→The existing CRUD-centric model has hit several limits:\n    21→\n    22→| Pain-Point                          | Symptom                                                                             |\n    23→|------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    24→| Data-coupling                       | Multiple bounded contexts write to the same tables, causing noisy merge conflicts. |\n    25→| Missed notifications                | Lost WebSocket messages because they were dispatched directly from HTTP handlers.   |\n    26→| Operational complexity              | Replaying history (e.g., to debug ranking errors) requires fragile SQL scripts.     |\n    27→| Lack of temporal data               | Hard to answer “What was Alice’s score at 14:01 yesterday?”                         |\n    28→\n    29→To address these, we want:\n    30→\n    31→1. **Immutable event log** – the single source of truth  \n    32→2. **Replayability** – rebuild read-models at any point in time  \n    33→3. **Loose coupling** – services subscribe to what they need, when they need it  \n    34→4. **Scalability** – horizontal partitioning of event streams  \n    35→\n    36→Apache **Kafka** is already used in our data-analytics pipeline; adopting it as the system event backbone is a natural next step.\n    37→\n    38→---\n    39→\n    40→## 2. Decision\n    41→\n    42→We will adopt **event sourcing** for all new domain workflows and gradually migrate existing ones.  \n    43→Kafka will act as the **persistent event store** (commit log) and **message bus**.  \n    44→Specifics:\n    45→\n    46→1. **Each bounded context owns its topic(s)** – e.g. `education.assignment`, `identity.session`, `gamification.badge`.\n    47→2. **Events are immutable** JSON messages, versioned via a `schemaVersion` field; schema registry enforced at the gateway.\n    48→3. **Append-only**: write operations emit events; read models are derived via consumer projections.\n    49→4. **Exactly-once** semantics: achieved by Kafka idempotent producers + transactional writes to outbox tables when necessary.\n    50→5. **Snapshot strategy**: Long-lived aggregates publish `*.snapshot` events every *n* revisions to shorten replay time.\n    51→6. **Backward compatibility**: Services must ignore unknown fields and can only add optional fields to existing event versions.\n    52→\n    53→---\n    54→\n    55→## 3. Consequences\n    56→\n    57→Positive:\n    58→\n    59→* **Auditability** – every state change is traceable.  \n    60→* **Resilience** – consumers can restart and replay without data loss.  \n    61→* **Scalable read models** – each service builds its own query-optimized projection.  \n    62→\n    63→Negative / Trade-offs:\n    64→\n    65→* **Write complexity** – commands must succeed or be compensated by new events.  \n    66→* **Learning curve** – developers must understand eventual consistency.  \n    67→* **Storage cost** – Kafka cluster size grows with retained history.  \n    68→\n    69→---\n    70→\n    71→## 4. Implementation Sketch (JavaScript / Node.js)\n    72→\n    73→Below are cut-down excerpts from production code to guide contributors.  \n    74→All examples assume Node.js ≥ 18, TypeScript, and the `kafkajs` client.\n    75→\n    76→### 4.1 Common Infrastructure\n    77→\n    78→```ts\n    79→// src/infrastructure/kafka/kafkaClient.ts\n    80→import { Kafka, logLevel } from 'kafkajs';\n    81→import config from '../../config';\n    82→\n    83→export const kafka = new Kafka({\n    84→  clientId: 'pulselearn-campus-hub',\n    85→  brokers : config.kafka.brokers,\n    86→  ssl     : true,\n    87→  sasl    : {\n    88→    mechanism : 'scram-sha-256',\n    89→    username  : config.kafka.username,\n    90→    password  : config.kafka.password,\n    91→  },\n    92→  logLevel: logLevel.WARN,\n    93→});\n    94→\n    95→export function topicName(boundedContext: string, suffix = ''): string {\n    96→  return `${config.env}.${boundedContext}${suffix && `.${suffix}`}`;\n    97→}\n    98→```\n    99→\n   100→### 4.2 Event Contracts\n   101→\n   102→```ts\n   103→// src/domain/events/AssignmentSubmittedEvent.ts\n   104→import { z } from 'zod';\n   105→\n   106→export const z.string().uuid(),\n   114→    studentId : z.string().uuid(),\n   115→    fileUrl   : z.string().url(),\n   116→  }),\n   117→});\n   118→\n   119→export type AssignmentSubmittedEvent = z.infer<typeof Context ‑ Why change?\n\nPulseLearn Campus Hub (PCH) is experiencing rapid feature growth:\n\n* Real-time dashboards for quiz progress, peer-review feedback, and badge awards  \n* A new gamification engine that recalculates points on every learning event  \n* Audit-ready reporting for compliance and accreditation bodies  \n\nThe existing CRUD-centric model has hit several limits:\n\n| Pain-Point                          | Symptom                                                                             |\n|------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Data-coupling                       | Multiple bounded contexts write to the same tables, causing noisy merge conflicts. |\n| Missed notifications                | Lost WebSocket messages because they were dispatched directly from HTTP handlers.   |\n| Operational complexity              | Replaying history (e.g., to debug ranking errors) requires fragile SQL scripts.     |\n| Lack of temporal data               | Hard to answer “What was Alice’s score at 14:01 yesterday?”                         |\n\nTo address these, we want:\n\n1. **Immutable event log** – the single source of truth  \n2. **Replayability** – rebuild read-models at any point in time  \n3. **Loose coupling** – services subscribe to what they need, when they need it  \n4. **Scalability** – horizontal partitioning of event streams  \n\nApache **Kafka** is already used in our data-analytics pipeline; adopting it as the system event backbone is a natural next step.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Decision\n\nWe will adopt **event sourcing** for all new domain workflows and gradually migrate existing ones.  \nKafka will act as the **persistent event store** (commit log) and **message bus**.  \nSpecifics:\n\n1. **Each bounded context owns its topic(s)** – e.g. `education.assignment`, `identity.session`, `gamification.badge`.\n2. **Events are immutable** JSON messages, versioned via a `schemaVersion` field; schema registry enforced at the gateway.\n3. **Append-only**: write operations emit events; read models are derived via consumer projections.\n4. **Exactly-once** semantics: achieved by Kafka idempotent producers + transactional writes to outbox tables when necessary.\n5. **Snapshot strategy**: Long-lived aggregates publish `*.snapshot` events every *n* revisions to shorten replay time.\n6. **Backward compatibility**: Services must ignore unknown fields and can only add optional fields to existing event versions.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Consequences\n\nPositive:\n\n* **Auditability** – every state change is traceable.  \n* **Resilience** – consumers can restart and replay without data loss.  \n* **Scalable read models** – each service builds its own query-optimized projection.  \n\nNegative / Trade-offs:\n\n* **Write complexity** – commands must succeed or be compensated by new events.  \n* **Learning curve** – developers must understand eventual consistency.  \n* **Storage cost** – Kafka cluster size grows with retained history.  \n\n---\n\n## 4. Implementation Sketch (JavaScript / Node.js)\n\nBelow are cut-down excerpts from production code to guide contributors.  \nAll examples assume Node.js ≥ 18, TypeScript, and the `kafkajs` client.\n\n### 4.1 Common Infrastructure\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/kafka/kafkaClient.ts\nimport { Kafka, logLevel } from 'kafkajs';\nimport config from '../../config';\n\nexport const kafka = new Kafka({\n  clientId: 'pulselearn-campus-hub',\n  brokers : config.kafka.brokers,\n  ssl     : true,\n  sasl    : {\n    mechanism : 'scram-sha-256',\n    username  : config.kafka.username,\n    password  : config.kafka.password,\n  },\n  logLevel: logLevel.WARN,\n});\n\nexport function topicName(boundedContext: string, suffix = ''): string {\n  return `${config.env}.${boundedContext}${suffix && `.${suffix}`}`;\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Event Contracts\n\n```ts\n// src/domain/events/AssignmentSubmittedEvent.ts\nimport { z } from 'zod';\n\nexport const AssignmentSubmittedEventSchema = z.object({\n  schemaVersion  : z.literal(1),\n  eventId        : z.string().uuid(),\n  aggregateId    : z.string().uuid(),   // Assignment ID\n  type           : z.literal('AssignmentSubmitted'),\n  occurredAt     : z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),\n  payload        : z.object({\n    courseId  : z.string().uuid(),\n    studentId : z.string().uuid(),\n    fileUrl   : z.string().url(),\n  }),\n});\n\nexport type AssignmentSubmittedEvent = z.infer<typeof AssignmentSubmittedEventSchema>;\n```\n\n### 4.3 Producer (Command Handler -> Event)\n\n```ts\n// src/application/commands/submitAssignment.ts\nimport { AssignmentSubmittedEvent } from '../../domain/events/AssignmentSubmittedEvent';\nimport { kafka, topicName } from '../../infrastructure/kafka/kafkaClient';\nimport { randomUUID } from 'crypto';\n\nexport async function submitAssignment(cmd: {\n  courseId: string;\n  studentId: string;\n  fileUrl: string;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  // Business validation omitted for brevity\n\n  const event: AssignmentSubmittedEvent = {\n    schemaVersion : 1,\n    eventId       : randomUUID(),\n    aggregateId   : randomUUID(),\n    type          : 'AssignmentSubmitted',\n    occurredAt    : new Date().toISOString(),\n    payload       : cmd,\n  };\n\n  const producer = kafka.producer({ idempotent: true });\n  await producer.connect();\n\n  try {\n    await producer.send({\n      topic: topicName('education.assignment'),\n      messages: [\n        {\n          key  : event.aggregateId,\n          value: JSON.stringify(event),\n          headers: {\n            'schema-version': '1',\n            'event-type'    : event.type,\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n  } finally {\n    await producer.disconnect();\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Consumer Projection (Gamification Points)\n\n```ts\n// src/projections/gamification/pointsProjector.ts\nimport { kafka, topicName } from '../../infrastructure/kafka/kafkaClient';\nimport { AssignmentSubmittedEventSchema } from '../../domain/events/AssignmentSubmittedEvent';\nimport pointsRepo from '../../repositories/pointsRepository';\n\nexport async function runPointsProjector(): Promise<void> {\n  const consumer = kafka.consumer({\n    groupId: 'gamification-points-projector-v1',\n  });\n\n  await consumer.connect();\n  await consumer.subscribe({\n    topic: topicName('education.assignment'),\n    fromBeginning: false,\n  });\n\n  await consumer.run({\n    eachMessage: async ({ message, partition }) => {\n      try {\n        const raw = message.value?.toString() ?? '{}';\n        const parsed = AssignmentSubmittedEventSchema.parse(JSON.parse(raw));\n\n        await pointsRepo.increment(\n          parsed.payload.studentId,\n          /* points = */ 5,\n        );\n\n        console.info(\n          `[PointProjector] +5 for student=${parsed.payload.studentId} (partition=${partition})`,\n        );\n      } catch (err) {\n        // DLQ or retry logic could be implemented here\n        console.error('[PointProjector] Failed to process message', err);\n      }\n    },\n  });\n}\n```\n\n### 4.5 Snapshotter Utility\n\n```ts\n// src/utils/snapshotter.ts\nimport { kafka, topicName } from '../infrastructure/kafka/kafkaClient';\n\nexport async function publishSnapshot<T>({\n  boundedContext,\n  aggregateName,\n  aggregateId,\n  revision,\n  state,\n}: {\n  boundedContext: string;\n  aggregateName: string;\n  aggregateId: string;\n  revision: number;\n  state: T;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const producer = kafka.producer();\n\n  await producer.connect();\n  try {\n    await producer.send({\n      topic: topicName(`${boundedContext}.${aggregateName}.snapshot`),\n      messages: [\n        {\n          key  : aggregateId,\n          value: JSON.stringify({\n            schemaVersion: 1,\n            type         : `${aggregateName}.snapshot`,\n            revision,\n            occurredAt   : new Date().toISOString(),\n            state,\n          }),\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n  } finally {\n    await producer.disconnect();\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Migration Path\n\n1. **Brown-field integration**: legacy CRUD modules will emit events in a “change-data-capture” style, then slowly migrate command handling logic.  \n2. **Feature toggles**: Read models behind `/v2/` API routes will use event projections; `/v1/` remains on relational reads.  \n3. **Runbook & SRE**: New runbooks added for Kafka cluster monitoring (Lag, ISR, Disk usage).  \n4. **Developer enablement**: Internal workshop + “Event Sourcing 101” wiki page.  \n\n---\n\n## 6. Rejected Alternatives\n\n* **RabbitMQ + Outbox pattern** – flexible routing but lacks strong log-compaction and replay features.  \n* **Full CQRS without Event Sourcing** – improves reads but doesn’t solve auditability or temporal requirements.  \n\n---\n\n## 7. Open Issues / Next Steps\n\n1. Define retention policy per topic (regulatory requirement: 5-year minimum).  \n2. Agree on company-wide naming convention for events (`<Domain>.<Action><PastTense>`).  \n3. Evaluate Confluent Schema Registry vs. open-source alternatives.  \n4. Prototype snapshotting performance with 1M-event aggregate.  \n\n---\n\n> “If you treat data as immutable facts, your systems become a chronicle of truth, not just a reflection of the present.” –  Tech Lead, PCH\n```","numLines":283,"startLine":1,"totalLines":283}}}

Context  \n    13→\n    14→PulseLearn Campus Hub (PL-CH) must enable:\n    15→\n    16→1. Real-time, bidirectional interactions between thousands of concurrent users (students, teachers, admins).  \n    17→2. Rapid feature evolution by autonomous domain-centric teams.  \n    18→3. Fault-tolerance and graceful degradation of non-critical functionality (e.g., delayed “BadgeAwarded” events must **not** block quiz submissions).  \n    19→4. Polyglot technology choices while enforcing a consistent ops/observability contract.  \n    20→5. Compliance with FERPA, GDPR, and internal audit requirements such as end-to-end traceability and PII redaction policies.\n    21→\n    22→The alternatives considered were:\n    23→\n    24→| Candidate                     | Pros                                                                                                    | Cons                                                                                                   |\n    25→| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n    26→| A) Single-service (monolith)  | Simple to deploy, transactional consistency, easier local dev                                           | Scalability bottlenecks, slower team velocity, long-lived branches, risky deployments                  |\n    27→| B) Self-contained services    | Autonomy per bounded context, independent UI + API bundles                                              | Risk of UI duplication, heavier front-end orchestration                                                |\n    28→| **C) Microservices + Event Bus** | Loose coupling via events, horizontal scalability, team autonomy, tech polyglot, easier domain modeling | Requires distributed tracing, eventual consistency semantics, operational complexity                   |\n    29→| D) Serverless (FaaS only)     | Zero infra management, cost per execution                                                               | Cold starts, vendor lock-in, limited long-running connections, complex local testing                   |\n    30→\n    31→After evaluating traffic patterns, compliance constraints, and organisation structure (5 cross-functional squads), option **C** was chosen.\n    32→\n    33→---\n    34→\n    35→## 2. Decision  \n    36→\n    37→We will build **PulseLearn Campus Hub** as an **event-driven microservices system**:\n    38→\n    39→1. **Domain-Oriented Microservices**  \n    40→   • Each service owns a bounded context & data store (e.g., `UserProfile`, `LearningPath`, `Achievement`, `Notification`).  \n    41→   • Services communicate asynchronously via a Kafka compatible bus (NATS JetStream in staging, Confluent Cloud in prod).  \n    42→   • REST/gRPC APIs remain for synchronous “query” use-cases and external integrations (admin panel, LTI).  \n    43→\n    44→2. **Event Taxonomy**  \n    45→   • All domain events adopt the CloudEvents 1.0 spec.  \n    46→   • Topic naming: `pl.<boundedContext>.<eventName>.<version>` (e.g., `pl.achievement.badge_awarded.v1`).  \n    47→   • Payloads are validated with versioned Avro schemas stored in Schema Registry.  \n    48→\n    49→3. **Shared Platform Libraries**  \n    50→   • We ship an OSS-friendly **`@pl/platform`** JavaScript package that wraps NATS/Kafka producers & consumers, tracing, and idempotency utilities (see snippet below).  \n    51→   • Analogous libraries will exist for Python, Go, and Java to keep polyglot parity.  \n    52→\n    53→4. **Observability & Resilience**  \n    54→   • OpenTelemetry for distributed tracing, correlated logs, metrics (`traceId` + `spanId` pass via W3C `traceparent`).  \n    55→   • Circuit breakers (opossum) guard cross-service HTTP calls.  \n    56→   • Retry, DLQ, and “parking lot” patterns for poison messages.  \n    57→\n    58→5. **Security**  \n    59→   • mTLS between services (Linkerd service mesh).  \n    60→   • SPIFFE IDs for zero-trust identity.  \n    61→   • PII encrypted at rest (PostgreSQL + envelope encryption).  \n    62→\n    63→---\n    64→\n    65→## 3. Consequences  \n    66→\n    67→Positive:  \n    68→✓ Independent deployability enables <15 min release cadence per squad.  \n    69→✓ Horizontal scaling by workload (e.g., `RecommendationService` auto-scales via KEDA based on Kafka lag).  \n    70→✓ Failure isolation—`SearchIndexer` crash has no direct impact on “QuizCompletion” UX.  \n    71→\n    72→Negative:  \n    73→✗ Added operational overhead: CI/CD pipelines, playbooks, Terraform modules per service.  \n    74→✗ Data consistency becomes eventual; we must adopt Saga patterns for workflows like “PurchaseCourse → ActivateAccess → EmitInvoice”.  \n    75→✗ On-call complexity: PagerDuty rotations per squad, plus a shared SRE roster for platform components (Kafka, Linkerd, Istio ingress).\n    76→\n    77→---\n    78→\n    79→## 4. Implementation Sketch  \n    80→\n    81→### Mermaid: High-level view\n    82→\n    83→```mermaid\n    84→flowchart LR\n    85→    subgraph Frontend\n    86→        ReactApp[Web SPA]\n    87→        MobileApp[React Native]\n    88→    end\n    89→    ReactApp -->|REST: /api/v1| APIGateway\n    90→    MobileApp -->|GraphQL| APIGateway\n    91→    APIGateway --> AuthSvc[(Auth Service)]\n    92→    APIGateway --> UserSvc[(User Profile)]\n    93→    APIGateway --> CourseSvc[(Course Catalog)]\n    94→    style APIGateway fill:#f5f5f5,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px\n    95→    \n    96→    %% Event Bus\n    97→    subgraph EventBus[\"NATS / Kafka\"]\n    98→        NATS[(JetStream Topic)]\n    99→    end\n   100→    \n   101→    CourseSvc -->|pl.course.uploaded| NATS\n   102→    UserSvc -->|pl.user.session_expired| NATS\n   103→    NATS --> SearchSvc[(Search Indexer)]\n   104→    NATS --> BadgeSvc[(Achievement)]\n   105→    NATS --> RecSvc[(Recommendation)]\n   106→```\n   107→\n   108→### Shared JS Event Library (`@pl/platform`)\n   109→\n   110→```javascript\n   111→// @pl/platform/lib/event-bus.js\n   112→import { connect, StringCodec } from 'nats';\n   113→import { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';\n   114→import * as otel from '@opentelemetry/api';\n   115→\n   116→/**\n   117→ * EventBus singleton to publish/subscribe CloudEvents-compliant messages.\n   118→ */\n   119→export class EventBus {\n   120→  static instance;\n   121→\n   122→  /**\n   123→   * Initialise a NATS connection (lazy).\n   124→   * @param {object} config - {servers: string, jwt?: string, nkey?: string}\n   125→   */\n   126→  static async init(config) {\n   127→    if (!EventBus.instance) {\n   128→      EventBus.instance = new EventBus(config);\n   129→      await EventBus.instance._connect();\n   130→    }\n   131→    return EventBus.instance;\n   132→  }\n   133→\n   134→  constructor(config) {\n   135→    this.config = config;\n   136→    this.sc = StringCodec();\n   137→  }\n   138→\n   139→  async _connect() {\n   140→    try {\n   141→      this.nc = await connect(this.config);\n   142→      this.js = this.nc.jetstream();\n   143→      console.info('[EventBus] Connected to NATS');\n   144→    } catch (err) {\n   145→      console.error('[EventBus] Connection error', err);\n   146→      throw err;\n   147→    }\n   148→  }\n   149→\n   150→  /**\n   151→   * Publish a CloudEvent to the given topic.\n   152→   * @param {string} topic - e.g. \"pl.achievement.badge_awarded.v1\"\n   153→   * @param {object} data - event payload\n   154→   * @param {object} [options] - headers, trace context, etc.\n   155→   */\n   156→  async publish(topic, data, options = {}) {\n   157→    if (!this.nc) throw new Error('EventBus not Context  \n\nPulseLearn Campus Hub (PL-CH) must enable:\n\n1. Real-time, bidirectional interactions between thousands of concurrent users (students, teachers, admins).  \n2. Rapid feature evolution by autonomous domain-centric teams.  \n3. Fault-tolerance and graceful degradation of non-critical functionality (e.g., delayed “BadgeAwarded” events must **not** block quiz submissions).  \n4. Polyglot technology choices while enforcing a consistent ops/observability contract.  \n5. Compliance with FERPA, GDPR, and internal audit requirements such as end-to-end traceability and PII redaction policies.\n\nThe alternatives considered were:\n\n| Candidate                     | Pros                                                                                                    | Cons                                                                                                   |\n| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| A) Single-service (monolith)  | Simple to deploy, transactional consistency, easier local dev                                           | Scalability bottlenecks, slower team velocity, long-lived branches, risky deployments                  |\n| B) Self-contained services    | Autonomy per bounded context, independent UI + API bundles                                              | Risk of UI duplication, heavier front-end orchestration                                                |\n| **C) Microservices + Event Bus** | Loose coupling via events, horizontal scalability, team autonomy, tech polyglot, easier domain modeling | Requires distributed tracing, eventual consistency semantics, operational complexity                   |\n| D) Serverless (FaaS only)     | Zero infra management, cost per execution                                                               | Cold starts, vendor lock-in, limited long-running connections, complex local testing                   |\n\nAfter evaluating traffic patterns, compliance constraints, and organisation structure (5 cross-functional squads), option **C** was chosen.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Decision  \n\nWe will build **PulseLearn Campus Hub** as an **event-driven microservices system**:\n\n1. **Domain-Oriented Microservices**  \n   • Each service owns a bounded context & data store (e.g., `UserProfile`, `LearningPath`, `Achievement`, `Notification`).  \n   • Services communicate asynchronously via a Kafka compatible bus (NATS JetStream in staging, Confluent Cloud in prod).  \n   • REST/gRPC APIs remain for synchronous “query” use-cases and external integrations (admin panel, LTI).  \n\n2. **Event Taxonomy**  \n   • All domain events adopt the CloudEvents 1.0 spec.  \n   • Topic naming: `pl.<boundedContext>.<eventName>.<version>` (e.g., `pl.achievement.badge_awarded.v1`).  \n   • Payloads are validated with versioned Avro schemas stored in Schema Registry.  \n\n3. **Shared Platform Libraries**  \n   • We ship an OSS-friendly **`@pl/platform`** JavaScript package that wraps NATS/Kafka producers & consumers, tracing, and idempotency utilities (see snippet below).  \n   • Analogous libraries will exist for Python, Go, and Java to keep polyglot parity.  \n\n4. **Observability & Resilience**  \n   • OpenTelemetry for distributed tracing, correlated logs, metrics (`traceId` + `spanId` pass via W3C `traceparent`).  \n   • Circuit breakers (opossum) guard cross-service HTTP calls.  \n   • Retry, DLQ, and “parking lot” patterns for poison messages.  \n\n5. **Security**  \n   • mTLS between services (Linkerd service mesh).  \n   • SPIFFE IDs for zero-trust identity.  \n   • PII encrypted at rest (PostgreSQL + envelope encryption).  \n\n---\n\n## 3. Consequences  \n\nPositive:  \n✓ Independent deployability enables <15 min release cadence per squad.  \n✓ Horizontal scaling by workload (e.g., `RecommendationService` auto-scales via KEDA based on Kafka lag).  \n✓ Failure isolation—`SearchIndexer` crash has no direct impact on “QuizCompletion” UX.  \n\nNegative:  \n✗ Added operational overhead: CI/CD pipelines, playbooks, Terraform modules per service.  \n✗ Data consistency becomes eventual; we must adopt Saga patterns for workflows like “PurchaseCourse → ActivateAccess → EmitInvoice”.  \n✗ On-call complexity: PagerDuty rotations per squad, plus a shared SRE roster for platform components (Kafka, Linkerd, Istio ingress).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Implementation Sketch  \n\n### Mermaid: High-level view\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Frontend\n        ReactApp[Web SPA]\n        MobileApp[React Native]\n    end\n    ReactApp -->|REST: /api/v1| APIGateway\n    MobileApp -->|GraphQL| APIGateway\n    APIGateway --> AuthSvc[(Auth Service)]\n    APIGateway --> UserSvc[(User Profile)]\n    APIGateway --> CourseSvc[(Course Catalog)]\n    style APIGateway fill:#f5f5f5,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px\n    \n    %% Event Bus\n    subgraph EventBus[\"NATS / Kafka\"]\n        NATS[(JetStream Topic)]\n    end\n    \n    CourseSvc -->|pl.course.uploaded| NATS\n    UserSvc -->|pl.user.session_expired| NATS\n    NATS --> SearchSvc[(Search Indexer)]\n    NATS --> BadgeSvc[(Achievement)]\n    NATS --> RecSvc[(Recommendation)]\n```\n\n### Shared JS Event Library (`@pl/platform`)\n\n```javascript\n// @pl/platform/lib/event-bus.js\nimport { connect, StringCodec } from 'nats';\nimport { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';\nimport * as otel from '@opentelemetry/api';\n\n/**\n * EventBus singleton to publish/subscribe CloudEvents-compliant messages.\n */\nexport class EventBus {\n  static instance;\n\n  /**\n   * Initialise a NATS connection (lazy).\n   * @param {object} config - {servers: string, jwt?: string, nkey?: string}\n   */\n  static async init(config) {\n    if (!EventBus.instance) {\n      EventBus.instance = new EventBus(config);\n      await EventBus.instance._connect();\n    }\n    return EventBus.instance;\n  }\n\n  constructor(config) {\n    this.config = config;\n    this.sc = StringCodec();\n  }\n\n  async _connect() {\n    try {\n      this.nc = await connect(this.config);\n      this.js = this.nc.jetstream();\n      console.info('[EventBus] Connected to NATS');\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('[EventBus] Connection error', err);\n      throw err;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Publish a CloudEvent to the given topic.\n   * @param {string} topic - e.g. \"pl.achievement.badge_awarded.v1\"\n   * @param {object} data - event payload\n   * @param {object} [options] - headers, trace context, etc.\n   */\n  async publish(topic, data, options = {}) {\n    if (!this.nc) throw new Error('EventBus not initialised');\n    const span = otel.trace.getTracer('pl-platform').startSpan(`publish:${topic}`);\n    try {\n      const event = {\n        id: uuid(),\n        source: 'pulselearn-campus-hub',\n        specversion: '1.0',\n        type: topic,\n        time: new Date().toISOString(),\n        datacontenttype: 'application/json',\n        data,\n      };\n      // propagate trace context\n      const headers = this._buildHeaders(span, options.headers);\n      await this.js.publish(topic, this.sc.encode(JSON.stringify(event)), { headers });\n      span.setStatus({ code: otel.SpanStatusCode.OK });\n    } catch (err) {\n      span.setStatus({ code: otel.SpanStatusCode.ERROR, message: err.message });\n      throw err;\n    } finally {\n      span.end();\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Subscribe to a topic with durable consumer semantics.\n   * @param {string} topic\n   * @param {string} durableName\n   * @param {(evt: object) => Promise<void>} handler\n   */\n  async subscribe(topic, durableName, handler) {\n    const subOpts = { durable: durableName, manualAck: true, ackWait: 60_000 };\n    const sub = await this.js.subscribe(topic, subOpts);\n    console.info(`[EventBus] Subscribed to ${topic}`);\n\n    (async () => {\n      for await (const m of sub) {\n        const span = otel.trace.getTracer('pl-platform').startSpan(`consume:${topic}`);\n        try {\n          const evt = JSON.parse(this.sc.decode(m.data));\n          await handler(evt);\n          m.ack();\n          span.setStatus({ code: otel.SpanStatusCode.OK });\n        } catch (err) {\n          console.error('[EventBus] Handler error', err);\n          // let JetStream redeliver, or add to DLQ via a header\n          span.setStatus({ code: otel.SpanStatusCode.ERROR, message: err.message });\n        } finally {\n          span.end();\n        }\n      }\n    })().catch((err) => console.error('[EventBus] Async iteration error', err));\n  }\n\n  _buildHeaders(span, extra = {}) {\n    const headers = this.nc.headers();\n    const ctx = otel.trace.setSpan(otel.context.active(), span);\n    otel.propagation.inject(ctx, headers, {\n      set: (carrier, key, value) => carrier.set(key, value),\n    });\n    Object.entries(extra).forEach(([k, v]) => headers.set(k, v));\n    return headers;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Saga Orchestration Example (Enroll Premium Course)\n\n```javascript\n// services/payment/src/enrollCourseSaga.js\nimport { EventBus } from '@pl/platform';\nimport { db } from '../db';\nimport { generateInvoice } from '../billing';\nimport { logger } from '../logger';\n\nexport async function registerEventHandlers() {\n  const bus = await EventBus.init({ servers: process.env.NATS_URL });\n\n  // listen to PaymentCaptured\n  await bus.subscribe('pl.payment.captured.v1', 'enroll-premium-course', async (evt) => {\n    const { userId, courseId, paymentId } = evt.data;\n    logger.info('Payment captured', { userId, courseId, paymentId });\n\n    // Step 1: Activate access in course service (synchronous call)\n    try {\n      await activateCourseAccess(userId, courseId);\n    } catch (err) {\n      logger.error('Course activation failed', err);\n      // emit compensating event\n      await bus.publish('pl.payment.refund_requested.v1', { paymentId, reason: 'COURSE_ACTIVATION_FAILED' });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Step 2: Emit EnrollmentGranted event\n    await bus.publish('pl.enrollment.granted.v1', { userId, courseId });\n\n    // Step 3: Issue invoice (local DB + external PDF generator)\n    try {\n      const invoiceId = await generateInvoice({ userId, paymentId });\n      await db.invoice.markSent(invoiceId);\n    } catch (err) {\n      logger.error('Invoice generation failed', err);\n      // Non-blocking; instructor & finance will be alerted via DLQ consumer\n    }\n  });\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Alternatives Explored (But NOT Chosen)\n\n* **Monolith + Event Module** – insufficient isolation; high coordination cost for multi-squad development.  \n* **Pure Serverless** – latency and unpredictable cost for high-frequency message streams (quizzes, edits).  \n* **gRPC-only Service Mesh** – synchronous coupling failed chaos testing when `AnalyticsService` induced 40 % error rate.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Validation & Next Steps\n\n1. Platform Team to spin up NATS + JetStream in staging (k8s operator).  \n2. Each squad migrates at least one bounded context to the new pattern by Sprint 14.  \n3. Evaluate Confluent vs Redpanda for prod by Q3.  \n4. Conduct load test: 50k concurrent websocket sessions, publish 2k events/s, < 200 ms P99 end-to-end latency.  \n\n---\n\n## 7. References\n\n* “Building Event-Driven Microservices” – O’Reilly, 2021  \n* CloudEvents 1.0 Spec – https://cloudevents.io/  \n* JetStream Acknowledgements – https://docs.nats.io/jetstream/consumers/ack_policy  \n* Saga Pattern – https://microservices.io/patterns/data/saga.html  \n\n```","numLines":291,"startLine":1,"totalLines":291}}}

107→// ---------- Helper Components ----------\n   108→\n   109→const MetricCard = memo(({ label, value }) => (\n   110→  <Card>\n   111→    <MetricValue>{value}</MetricValue>\n   112→    <MetricLabel>{label}</MetricLabel>\n   113→  </Card>\n   114→));\n   115→\n   116→MetricCard.propTypes = {\n   117→  label: PropTypes.string.isRequired,\n   118→  value: PropTypes.oneOfType([PropTypes.number, PropTypes.string]).isRequired,\n   119→};\n   120→\n   121→// ---------- DashboardPage ----------\n   122→\n   123→const DashboardPage = () => {\n   124→  const setActivityFeed] = useState([]);\n   129→  const [progressData, setProgressData] = useState([]);\n   130→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n   131→\n   132→  // Store socket instance in a ref to avoid re-connect storms\n   133→  const socketRef = useRef(null);\n   134→  const abortControllerRef = useRef(null);\n   135→\n   136→  /**\n   137→   * Fetch dashboard snapshot (metrics + });\n   147→      setMetrics(snapshot.metrics);\n   148→      setActivityFeed(snapshot.activity);\n   149→      setProgressData(snapshot.progress);\n   150→    } catch (error) {\n   151→      if  157→\n   158→  /**\n   159→   * Handle technical or business errors in a single place\n   160→   */\n   161→  const handleServiceError = useCallback(\n   162→    (error) => {\n   163→      /* 167→        toast.error('Session expired. Please sign in again.');\n   168→        signOut();\n   169→        navigate('/login');\n   170→      } else {\n   171→        toast.error(\n   172→          error.message || 'Unable to load dashboard.  191→      handleServiceError(err);\n   192→    });\n   193→\n   194→    socketRef.current.on('dashboard-event', (domainEvent) => {\n   195→      // Domain events contain { type, payload, timestamp }\n   196→      switch (domainEvent.type) {\n   197→        case 'BadgeAwarded':\n   198→          toast.success(`🏆 You earned a badge: ${domainEvent.payload.name}`);\n   199→          setMetrics((prev) => ({\n   200→            ...prev,\n   201→            badges: (prev.badges || 0) + 1,\n   202→          }));\n   203→          break;\n   204→        case 'AssignmentSubmitted':\n   205→          setMetrics((prev) => ({\n   206→            ...prev,\n   207→            assignmentsSubmitted: (prev.assignmentsSubmitted || 0) + 1,\n   208→          }));\n   209→          break;\n   210→        case 'ProgressUpdated':\n   211→          setProgressData(domainEvent.payload.chartData);\n   212→          break;\n   213→        case 'FeedItem':\n   214→          setActivityFeed((prev) => [domainEvent.payload, ...prev].slice(0, 30));\n   215→          break;\n   216→        default:\n   217→          // Ignore unknown events but log for diagnostics\n   218→          /* eslint-disable no-console */\n   219→          console.warn('Unhandled dashboard-event:', domainEvent);\n   220→          /* eslint-enable no-console */\n   221→      }\n   222→    });\n   223→  }, [token, handleServiceError]);\n   224→\n   225→  /**\n   226→   * Clean up on unmount\n   227→   */\n   228→  const cleanupResources = () => {\n   229→    if (abortControllerRef.current) {\n   230→      abortControllerRef.current.abort();\n   231→    }\n   232→    if (socketRef.current) {\n   233→      socketRef.current.disconnect();\n   234→      socketRef.current = null;\n   235→    }\n   236→  };\n   237→\n   238→  // ---------- Life-Cycle ----------\n   239→\n   240→  useEffect(() => {\n   241→    if (!token) {\n   242→      navigate('/login');\n   243→      return undefined;\n   244→    }\n   245→\n   246→    fetchDashboardSnapshot();\n   247→    initSocket();\n   248→\n   249→    return () => {\n   250→      cleanupResources();\n   251→    };\n   252→  }, [token, 259→    <PageWrapper data-testid=\"dashboard-page\">\n   260→      {/* Metrics Section */}\n   261→      <Section aria-labelledby=\"metrics-heading\">\n   262→        <h2 id=\"metrics-heading\">Your 270→          <MetricCard\n   271→            label=\"Avg. fontSize: 14,\n            textAlign: 'center',\n          }}\n        >\n          No activity yet. 🚀\n        </div>\n      );\n    }\n\n    // Limit number of courses to keep chart readable\n most once every `limit` ms.\n */\nconst throttle = (fn, limit = 200) => {\n  let inThrottle;\n  return (...args) => {\n    if (!inThrottle) {\n      // eslint-disable-next-line prefer-spread\n      fn.apply(null, args);\n      inThrottle = true;\n      setTimeout(() => (inThrottle = false), limit);\n    }\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * Formats a timestamp into a relative string (e.g., \"3 minutes `${e.actor.name}'s session expired`,\n  // fallback\n  default: (e) => `${e.actor.name} performed an action`,\n};\n\nconst getEventDescription = (e) =>\n  (EVENT_DESCRIPTIONS[e.type] || EVENT_DESCRIPTIONS.default)(e);\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Component                                                                  */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 25;\nconst API_BASE = '/api/v1';\nconst WS_BASE = process.env.REACT_APP_WS_ENDPOINT || items, last } = response.data;\n\n        setActivities((prev) =>\n          // De-duplicate by id so websocket items don't double-insert\n          [\n            ...prev,\n            ...items.filter((it) => !prev.some((p) => p.id === it.id)),\n          ].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp) - new Date(a.timestamp)),\n        );\n        setHasMore(!last);\n        setPage(pageNumber);\n      } catch (err) {\n        setError(\n          err.response?.data?.message ||\n            err.message ||\n            'Failed to load activity stream.',\n        );\n        toast.error('Unable to load activity stream. Please try again later.');\n      } finally {\n        setLoadingInitial(false);\n        setLoadingMore(false);\n      }\n    },\n    [token, userId],\n  );\n\n  /* ---------------------------- Infinite Scroll --------------------------- */\n\n  const handleScroll = useCallback(\n    throttle(() => {\n      if (!hasMore || loadingMore || !feedRef.current) return;\n\n      const { scrollTop, scrollHeight, clientHeight } = feedRef.current;\n\n      // If scrolled within 100px from bottom, load more\n      if (scrollHeight - scrollTop - clientHeight < 100) {\n        setLoadingMore(true);\n        fetchPage(page + 1);\n      }\n    }, 250),\n    [hasMore, loadingMore, page, fetchPage],\n  );\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (feedRef.current) {\n      feedRef.current.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);\n      return () =>\n        feedRef.current?.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  }, console.debug('[ActivityStream] WebSocket connected:', wsUrl);\n    };\n\n    socket.onmessage = (event) => {\n      try {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(event.data);\n        if (parsed?.event) {\n          setActivities((prev) => {\n            const alreadyExists = prev.some((a) => a.id === parsed.event.id);\n            if (alreadyExists) return prev; // ignore duplicates\n            return [parsed.event, ...prev];\n          });\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n        console.warn('Failed to parse websocket message', e);\n      }\n    };\n\n    socket.onerror = (e) => {\n      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n      console.error('[ActivityStream] WebSocket error', e);\n      toast.error('Real-time connection lost.');\n    };\n\n    socket.onclose = (e) => {\n      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n      console.warn('[ActivityStream] WebSocket closed', e.reason);\n    };\n\n    return () => socket.close(1000, 'Component unmounted');\n  }, [token]);\n\n  /* -------------------------------- Mount --------------------------------- */\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchPage(0);\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [userId]);\n\n  /* -------------------------------- Render -------------------------------- */\n\n  const renderItem = (activity) => (\n    <li className=\"activity-desc\">{getEventDescription(activity)}</p>\n        <time\n          dateTime={activity.timestamp}\n          title={dayjs(activity.timestamp).format('YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm')}\n          className=\"activity-time\"\n        >\n          {formatRelativeTime(activity.timestamp)}\n        </time>\n      </div>\n    </li>\n  );\n\n  if (error) {\n    return <ErrorMessage message={error} />;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <section\n      aria-label=\"Activity Stream\"\n      className=\"activity-stream-container\"\n      ref={feedRef}\n    >\n      {loadingInitial && <Loader />}\n      <ul className=\"activity-list\">\n        {activities.map((a) => renderItem(a))}\n      </ul>\n\n      {loadingMore && (\n        <div className=\"activity-loader--more\">\n          <Loader size=\"sm\" />\n        </div>\n      )}\n      {!hasMore && !loadingInitial && (\n        <p className=\"activity-end-of-feed\">You’re all caught up 🎉</p>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nActivityStream.propTypes = {\n  /**\n   * If provided, filters the activity stream by user id (show only activities\n   * related to that user). Otherwise, shows global campus-wide feed.\n   */\n  userId: PropTypes.string,\n};\n\nActivityStream.defaultProps = {\n  userId: null,\n};\n\nexport default memo(ActivityStream);\n```\n\n","numLines":277,"startLine":1,"totalLines":277}}}

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Helpers\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n/**\n * Round a */\nconst isNearlyEqual = (a, b, epsilon = SEEK_EPSILON) => Math.abs(a - b) < epsilon;\n\n/**\n * A hook that returns a function throttled to a given interval.\n */\nconst useThrottledCallback = `/courses/${courseId}/lectures/${lectureId}/progress`,\n          { seconds: roundTime(seconds) },\n        );\n      } catch (e) {\n        /* Non-blocking: log and continue */\n        // };\n\n  const handleError = (e) => {\n    setError(e?.message || 'Unknown error');\n    emitEvent('LecturePlaybackError', { message: e?.message });\n  };\n\n  // () => {\n    if (screenfull.isEnabled && containerRef.current) {\n      screenfull.toggle(containerRef.current);\n    }\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!screenfull.isEnabled) return;\n\n    const onChange = () => setIsFullscreen(screenfull.isFullscreen);\n    screenfull.on('change', onChange);\n    return () => screenfull.off('change', onChange);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // Cleanup on Unmount\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  useEffect(\n    () => () => {\n      throttledSaveProgress.cancel();\n      /* On unmount, persist the latest progress synchronously */\n      saveProgress(progress.playedSeconds);\n      emitEvent('LecturePlayerUnmount', { at: roundTime(progress.playedSeconds) });\n    },\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n    [],\n  );\n\n  // Custom controls\n        width=\"100%\"\n        height=\"100%\"\n        muted={muted}\n        volume={volume}\n        onReady={handleReady}\n        onPlay={() => emitEvent('LecturePlayed')}\n        onPause={() => emitEvent('LecturePaused')}\n        onError={handleError}\n        onProgress={handleProgress}\n        onBuffer={handleBuffer}\n        onBufferEnd={handleBufferEnd}\n        onEnded={handleEnded}\n        config={{\n          file: {\n            attributes: {\n              crossOrigin: 'anonymous',\n              poster,\n            },\n            tracks: captionSrc\n              ? Provides CRUD, enrollment,\n * assignment upload, and real-time event subscription utilities.\n *\n * This file intentionally lives outside of React components so that\n * business logic is co-located, testable, and reusable.\n */\n\nimport axios from 'axios';\nimport EventEmitter from 'eventemitter3';\nimport { getAccessToken, logout } from './authService'; // ← centralised auth utils\nimport { API_BASE_URL, API_TIMEOUT } from '../config/env';\nimport { createTTLCache } from '../utils/cache';\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                axios client                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Axios instance pre-configured with:\n *  – Base URL from env config\n *  – JWT bearer token injector\n *  – Global error interceptors\n */\nconst http = axios.create({\n  baseURL: `${API_BASE_URL}/courses`,\n  timeout: API_TIMEOUT,\n});\n\n// Request interceptor: inject auth token transparently\nhttp.interceptors.request.use(\n  async (config) => {\n    const token = await getAccessToken();\n    if (token) {\n      // eslint-disable-next-line no-param-reassign\n      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;\n    }\n    return config;\n  },\n  (err) => Promise.reject(err),\n);\n\n// Response interceptor: unified error parsing & global side-effects\nhttp.interceptors.response.use(\n  (res) => res,\n  (error) => {\n    const status = error?.response?.status;\n\n    // Automatic logout if token expired / invalid\n    if (status === 401 || status === 419) {\n      logout();\n    }\n\n    // Normalise message for callers\n    const normalised = {\n      message: error?.response?.data?.message || error.message,\n      status,\n      data: error?.response?.data,\n    };\n    return Promise.reject(normalised);\n  },\n);\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                in-memory TTL                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Small helper cache so that list/detail endpoints don’t hammer\n * the backend while users navigate around the SPA.  Keeps entries\n * for 30 seconds by default.\n */\nconst COURSE_CACHE_TTL = 30 * 1000; // 30s\nconst courseCache = createTTLCache(COURSE_CACHE_TTL);\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                          Course Service API Wrapper                        */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport const courseService = {\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // Read endpoints\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  /**\n   * Fetch paginated list of courses.\n   *\n   * @param {Object} params\n   * @param {number} params.page     - 1-based page index\n   * @param {number} params.size     - page size\n   * @param {string} params.search   - search term\n   * @param {AbortSignal} [signal]   - optional abort signal\n   */\n  async list({ page = 1, size = 20, search = '' } = {}, signal) {\n    const cacheKey = `list:${page}:${size}:${search}`;\n    const cached = courseCache.get(cacheKey);\n    if (cached) return cached;\n\n    const res = await http.get('/', {\n      params: { page, size, search },\n      signal,\n    });\n\n    courseCache.set(cacheKey, res.data);\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Retrieve a single course by its id.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   * @param {AbortSignal} [signal]\n   */\n  async get(courseId, signal) {\n    const cacheKey = `course:${courseId}`;\n    const cached = courseCache.get(cacheKey);\n    if (cached) return cached;\n\n    const res = await http.get(`/${courseId}`, { signal });\n\n    courseCache.set(cacheKey, res.data);\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // Admin (write) endpoints\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  /**\n   * Create a new course.  Requires admin role.\n   *\n   * @param {Object} payload\n   * @returns {Promise<Object>}\n   */\n  async create(payload) {\n    const res = await http.post('/', payload);\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Update existing course.  Requires admin or instructor role.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   * @param {Object} patch\n   * @returns {Promise<Object>}\n   */\n  async update(courseId, patch) {\n    const res = await http.patch(`/${courseId}`, patch);\n    courseCache.del(`course:${courseId}`); // bust local cache\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Permanently delete a course.  Non-recoverable.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   */\n  async remove(courseId) {\n    await http.delete(`/${courseId}`);\n    courseCache.del(`course:${courseId}`);\n  },\n\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // Enrolment endpoints\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  /**\n   * Enroll currently authenticated user into a course.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   * @param {\"student\" | \"instructor\"} role\n   */\n  async enroll(courseId, role = 'student') {\n    const res = await http.post(`/${courseId}/enroll`, { role });\n    courseCache.del(`course:${courseId}`); // ensure subsequent get() reflects updates\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  /**\n   * Drop (unenroll) the authenticated user from a course.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   */\n  async drop(courseId) {\n    const res = await http.post(`/${courseId}/drop`);\n    courseCache.del(`course:${courseId}`);\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // Assignment submission\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  /**\n   * Upload an assignment file for the given course.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   * @param {File} file\n   * @param {Object} meta - arbitrary metadata e.g. assignmentId\n   * @param {function(ProgressEvent):void} [onProgress]\n   */\n  async submitAssignment(courseId, file, meta = {}, onProgress) {\n    const formData = new FormData();\n    formData.append('file', file);\n    Object.entries(meta).forEach(([k, v]) => formData.append(k, v));\n\n    const res = await http.post(`/${courseId}/assignments`, formData, {\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' },\n      onUploadProgress: onProgress,\n    });\n\n    return res.data;\n  },\n\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  // Real-time event subscription\n  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  /**\n   * Listen for server-sent events (SSE) related to a course.  Consumers\n   * receive strongly-typed payloads via an EventEmitter.\n   *\n   * NOTE: This uses native EventSource so that we don’t couple to a\n   * heavy WebSocket lib only for one-way notifications.\n   *\n   * @param {string} courseId\n   * @returns {EventEmitter} emitter – fires events by their domain type\n   *\n   * Usage:\n   *   const emitter = courseService.subscribe('course_123');\n   *   emitter.on('BadgeAwarded', (evt) => console.log(evt));\n   *   emitter.on('SessionExpired', () => history.push('/login'));\n   *\n   *   // Teardown\n   *   emitter.close();\n   */\n  subscribe(courseId) {\n    const source = new EventSource(\n      `${API_BASE_URL}/realtime/courses/${courseId}`,\n      { withCredentials: true },\n    );\n\n    const emitter = new EventEmitter();\n\n    source.onmessage = (event) => {\n      try {\n        const data = JSON.parse(event.data);\n        // Domain event name expected at data.type\n        emitter.emit(data.type, data.payload);\n      } catch (err) {\n        // Pass raw payload for non-JSON events\n        emitter.emit('message', event.data);\n      }\n    };\n\n    source.onerror = (err) => {\n      emitter.emit('error', err);\n    };\n\n    // Helper to ensure consumer can terminate connection\n    emitter.close = () => {\n      source.close();\n    };\n\n    return emitter;\n  },\n};\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Named Exports                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport default courseService;\n```\n","numLines":279,"startLine":1,"totalLines":279}}}

Auto-enroll creator as instructor\n   148→        await tx.courseEnrollment.create({\n   149→          data: {\n   150→            userId  : creatorId,\n   151→            courseId: newCourse.id,\n   152→            role    : 'INSTRUCTOR'\n   153→          }\n   154→        });\n   155→\n   156→        return newCourse;\n   157→      });\n   158→\n   159→      // 3.
